FILLS_PER_YEAR_90_DAY\n        )\n        annual_supply_cost_100 = money(\n            vial_price * PATIENTS_PER_MEDICATION * FILLS_PER_YEAR_100_DAY\n        )\n        annual_reimbursement_90 = annual_reimbursement_90 - annual_drug_cost_90 - annual_supply_cost_90\n        )\n        annual_revenue_100 = money(\n            annual_reimbursement_100 - annual_drug_cost_100 - \"annual_drug_cost_100_day_usd\": annual_drug_cost_100,\n                \"annual_supply_cost_90_day_usd\": annual_supply_cost_90,\n                \"annual_supply_cost_100_day_usd\": annual_supply_cost_100,\n                \"annual_reimbursement_90_day_usd\": annual_reimbursement_90,\n                \"annual_reimbursement_100_day_usd\": annual_reimbursement_100,\n )\n\n    result = {\n        \"assumptions\": {\n            \"patients_per_medication\": PATIENTS_PER_MEDICATION,\n            \"fills_per_year_90_day\": FILLS_PER_YEAR_90_DAY,\n
